# Winners matrix, b200

Generated by `tools/winners.py` from the phase 3 batches under `state/results/eval-engineer/phase3/b200`. Nodes: umbriel-b200-047, umbriel-b200-068, umbriel-b200-094. Harness: 0.10, 0.11. Latency in cycles per call, throughput in Gelem/s. Margin over the runner-up and over the reference control measured in the same lock section (latency: how much slower the other is, throughput: how much faster the winner is). Cells with a margin over the runner-up below 15 percent are listed as close and get the tie_heavy pass.

Reading notes. (1) Every value is from the harness version named above; cards made with earlier versions differ by the documented control changes (harness/README.md section 3.1), for example B4_v4 against B3_tuned at fp32 N 16384 reads 0.78 to 0.80 of the card ratio under 0.10. (2) Tables whose input pattern carries `@runsM` are the sorted-runs track (chunk merges on M presorted runs): only adapters measured on that input appear there, a single-candidate row has no runner-up, and these rows are not head-to-head with the random-input tables. (3) A runner-up marked `(spills)` has register spills or local memory at that cell, so its margin is against a degraded form (B2_packed above 32 items per thread is the known case). (4) When a table's cells were measured on more than one node the `node` column says which, candidates of one cell always share a node and a session. (5) N8_b2packed is B2_packed launched at 128 threads and is reported under the B2_packed name with the `@T128` suffix, so the same kernel never appears twice in a cell. (7) Tables whose pattern carries `@sorted` are the phase 3b pass (sorted-output oracle, a different code path in some adapters), tables with `@addendum:<node>` were measured on another node than the main random pass and are not mixed into its one-node tables, and on the sorted-runs track an adapter named with --runs-reference (B1_det3, an unsorted selection over presorted runs) is a reference column, not a candidate. (6) The 3b sorted-output section at the end lists, per adapter, the cells that passed the order check over the full oracle battery, the cells unsupported on the device (oracle kernel could not launch) and the cells that failed the order check, with the reading per adapter.
